===== BEGIN src/nvda_desk/vocabulary_synonym_audit.py =====
     1	"""Deterministic vocabulary synonym-candidate audit.
     2	
     3	This audit does not call external APIs. It writes a structured escalation artefact
     4	that a later LLM pass in the sandbox can consume.
     5	"""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import argparse
    10	import hashlib
    11	import json
    12	import re
    13	from pathlib import Path
    14	
    15	from nvda_desk.schemas.vocabulary import (
    16	    VocabularyCandidate,
    17	    VocabularyCandidateAction,
    18	    VocabularyCandidateClassification,
    19	    vocabulary_document_from_dict,
    20	)
    21	from nvda_desk.vocabulary_builder import build_canonical_payload, governed_source_hashes
    22	
    23	OUTPUT_PATH = Path("var/vocabulary/synonym_candidates.json")
    24	STAMP_PATH = Path("data/vocabulary/vocabulary_touch_stamp.json")
    25	SCAN_PATHS = [Path("src"), Path("tests"), Path("docs"), Path("config")]
    26	ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {".py", ".md", ".yaml", ".yml", ".json", ".toml"}
    27	TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_\- ]{2,80}")
    28	
    29	
    30	def _normalise(value: str) -> str:
    31	    return re.sub(r"[_\-\s]+", " ", value.strip().casefold())
    32	
    33	
    34	def _compact_normalise(value: str) -> str:
    35	    return re.sub(r"[_\-\s]+", "", value.strip().casefold())
    36	
    37	
    38	def _fingerprint() -> str:
    39	    items = governed_source_hashes()
    40	    blob = json.dumps(items, sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
    41	    return hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest()
    42	
    43	
    44	def _load_document() -> dict:
    45	    payload = build_canonical_payload()
    46	    vocabulary_document_from_dict(payload)
    47	    return payload
    48	
    49	
    50	def _lexicon(payload: dict) -> tuple[set[str], set[str], list[tuple[str, str, str, str]]]:
    51	    exact_tokens: set[str] = set()
    52	    compact_exact_tokens: set[str] = set()
    53	    entries: list[tuple[str, str, str, str]] = []
    54	    for entry in payload["entries"]:
    55	        label = entry["canonical_label"]
    56	        slug = entry["canonical_slug"]
    57	        canonical_norm = _normalise(label)
    58	        for token in [label, slug]:
    59	            exact_tokens.add(_normalise(token))
    60	            compact_exact_tokens.add(_compact_normalise(token))
    61	        for alias in entry.get("allowed_aliases", []):
    62	            exact_tokens.add(_normalise(alias))
    63	            compact_exact_tokens.add(_compact_normalise(alias))
    64	        for phrase in entry.get("disallowed_phrases", []):
    65	            exact_tokens.add(_normalise(phrase))
    66	            compact_exact_tokens.add(_compact_normalise(phrase))
    67	        entries.append((slug, label, canonical_norm, entry.get("category", "")))
    68	    return exact_tokens, compact_exact_tokens, entries
    69	
    70	
    71	def _iter_candidates():
    72	    for base in SCAN_PATHS:
    73	        if not base.exists():
    74	            continue
    75	        for path in base.rglob("*"):
    76	            if not path.is_file() or path.suffix not in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS:
    77	                continue
    78	            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
    79	            for match in TOKEN_PATTERN.finditer(text):
    80	                yield str(path), match.group(0)
    81	
    82	
    83	def _is_identifier_like(raw_phrase: str) -> bool:
    84	    return (
    85	        " " not in raw_phrase
    86	        and "_" not in raw_phrase
    87	        and "-" not in raw_phrase
    88	        and raw_phrase != raw_phrase.lower()
    89	    )
    90	
    91	
    92	def _is_protocol_identifier_fragment(
    93	    raw_phrase: str,
    94	    candidate: str,
    95	    target: str,
    96	    category: str,
    97	) -> bool:
    98	    if category != "protocol_field" or "_" not in raw_phrase:
    99	        return False
   100	    candidate_parts = candidate.split()
   101	    target_parts = target.split()
   102	    if not set(target_parts) <= set(candidate_parts):
   103	        return False
   104	    extra_parts = set(candidate_parts) - set(target_parts)
   105	    return len(extra_parts) <= 1
   106	
   107	
   108	def _is_python_function_declaration_identifier_fragment(
   109	    path: str,
   110	    raw_phrase: str,
   111	    candidate: str,
   112	    target: str,
   113	) -> bool:
   114	    if not path.endswith(".py") or "_" not in raw_phrase:
   115	        return False
   116	    if not raw_phrase.lstrip().startswith("def "):
   117	        return False
   118	    candidate_parts = candidate.split()
   119	    target_parts = target.split()
   120	    if candidate_parts[:1] != ["def"]:
   121	        return False
   122	    return candidate_parts[1:] == target_parts
   123	
   124	
   125	
   126	def _candidate_slug_from_phrase(phrase: str) -> str:
   127	    slug = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", phrase.strip().casefold()).strip("_")
   128	    if not slug:
   129	        raise ValueError("candidate phrase must yield a non-empty slug")
   130	    return slug
   131	
   132	
   133	def _escalation_classification(phrase: str, nearest_slug: str) -> dict[str, str | None]:
   134	    return VocabularyCandidateClassification(
   135	        action=VocabularyCandidateAction.QUARANTINE,
   136	        candidate_slug=_candidate_slug_from_phrase(phrase),
   137	        matched_slug=nearest_slug,
   138	        reason="near-match synonym candidate requires LLM escalation before vocabulary admission",
   139	    ).to_dict()
   140	
   141	
   142	def _escalation_admission_review(phrase: str, nearest_slug: str) -> dict:
   143	    candidate = VocabularyCandidate(
   144	        candidate_slug=_candidate_slug_from_phrase(phrase),
   145	        candidate_label=phrase,
   146	    )
   147	    classification = VocabularyCandidateClassification(
   148	        action=VocabularyCandidateAction.QUARANTINE,
   149	        candidate_slug=candidate.candidate_slug,
   150	        matched_slug=nearest_slug,
   151	        reason="near-match synonym candidate requires LLM escalation before vocabulary admission",
   152	    )
   153	    return {
   154	        "status": "quarantined",
   155	        "candidate_slug": candidate.candidate_slug,
   156	        "plan": {
   157	            "classification": classification.to_dict(),
   158	            "proposed_entry": None,
   159	            "alias_candidates": [],
   160	            "quarantine_reason": classification.reason,
   161	        },
   162	        "error": classification.reason,
   163	    }
   164	
   165	
   166	def _candidate_score(candidate: str, target: str) -> int:
   167	    c_parts = candidate.split()
   168	    t_parts = target.split()
   169	    if candidate == target:
   170	        return 100
   171	    if candidate.replace(" ", "") == target.replace(" ", ""):
   172	        return 98
   173	    if len(c_parts) == len(t_parts) and set(c_parts) == set(t_parts):
   174	        return 95
   175	    if abs(len(c_parts) - len(t_parts)) == 1:
   176	        smaller = set(c_parts) if len(c_parts) < len(t_parts) else set(t_parts)
   177	        larger = set(c_parts) if len(c_parts) > len(t_parts) else set(t_parts)
   178	        if smaller <= larger and len(smaller) >= 2:
   179	            return 93
   180	    return 0
   181	
   182	
   183	def build_report() -> dict:
   184	    payload = _load_document()
   185	    exact_tokens, compact_exact_tokens, entries = _lexicon(payload)
   186	    fingerprint = _fingerprint()
   187	    previous_stamp = {}
   188	    if STAMP_PATH.exists():
   189	        previous_stamp = json.loads(STAMP_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   190	    current_touched = previous_stamp.get("fingerprint") != fingerprint
   191	
   192	    candidates: list[dict] = []
   193	    seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
   194	    for path, raw_phrase in _iter_candidates():
   195	        if _is_identifier_like(raw_phrase):
   196	            continue
   197	        phrase = _normalise(raw_phrase)
   198	        compact_phrase = _compact_normalise(raw_phrase)
   199	        if len(phrase) < 8 or phrase in exact_tokens or compact_phrase in compact_exact_tokens:
   200	            continue
   201	        best = None
   202	        best_score = 0
   203	        for slug, label, target, category in entries:
   204	            score = _candidate_score(phrase, target)
   205	            if score:
   206	                if _is_protocol_identifier_fragment(raw_phrase, phrase, target, category):
   207	                    score = 0
   208	                if _is_python_function_declaration_identifier_fragment(
   209	                    path, raw_phrase, phrase, target
   210	                ):
   211	                    score = 0
   212	            if score > best_score:
   213	                best = (slug, label)
   214	                best_score = score
   215	        if best is None or best_score < 93:
   216	            continue
   217	        key = (path, phrase)
   218	        if key in seen:
   219	            continue
   220	        seen.add(key)
   221	        candidates.append(
   222	            {
   223	                "phrase": phrase,
   224	                "nearest_slug": best[0],
   225	                "nearest_label": best[1],
   226	                "score": best_score,
   227	                "path": path,
   228	                "resolution_required": "llm_escalation",
   229	            }
   230	        )
   231	
   232	    sorted_candidates = sorted(
   233	        candidates, key=lambda item: (-item["score"], item["path"], item["phrase"])
   234	    )
   235	    return {
   236	        "schema_version": "v1",
   237	        "trigger": "vocabulary_governance",
   238	        "vocabulary_touched": current_touched,
   239	        "fingerprint": fingerprint,
   240	        "candidates": sorted_candidates,
   241	        "candidate_classifications": [
   242	            {
   243	                "phrase": item["phrase"],
   244	                "path": item["path"],
   245	                "nearest_slug": item["nearest_slug"],
   246	                "classification": _escalation_classification(
   247	                    str(item["phrase"]), str(item["nearest_slug"])
   248	                ),
   249	                "admission_review": _escalation_admission_review(
   250	                    str(item["phrase"]), str(item["nearest_slug"])
   251	                ),
   252	            }
   253	            for item in sorted_candidates
   254	        ],
   255	    }
   256	
   257	
   258	def write_report() -> dict:
   259	    report = build_report()
   260	    OUTPUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   261	    OUTPUT_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
   262	    return report
   263	
   264	
   265	def write_stamp() -> None:
   266	    report = build_report()
   267	    if report["candidates"]:
   268	        raise RuntimeError(
   269	            "cannot update vocabulary touch stamp while synonym candidates require LLM escalation"
   270	        )
   271	    STAMP_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   272	    STAMP_PATH.write_text(
   273	        json.dumps({"fingerprint": report["fingerprint"]}, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
   274	    )
   275	
   276	
   277	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
   278	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
   279	    parser.add_argument(
   280	        "--check-clean",
   281	        action="store_true",
   282	        help="write the audit report and fail if unresolved synonym candidates exist",
   283	    )
   284	    parser.add_argument(
   285	        "--write-stamp",
   286	        action="store_true",
   287	        help="advance the vocabulary touch stamp only when the audit is clean",
   288	    )
   289	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   290	
   291	    if args.write_stamp:
   292	        write_stamp()
   293	        print("vocabulary_touch_stamp=UPDATED")
   294	        return 0
   295	
   296	    report = write_report()
   297	    candidate_count = len(report["candidates"])
   298	    print(
   299	        "vocabulary_synonym_audit=PASS"
   300	        f" candidates={candidate_count}"
   301	        f" vocabulary_touched={report['vocabulary_touched']}"
   302	    )
   303	    if args.check_clean and candidate_count:
   304	        print("vocabulary_synonym_audit=BLOCKED unresolved_candidates=" + str(candidate_count))
   305	        return 1
   306	    return 0
   307	
   308	
   309	if __name__ == "__main__":
   310	    raise SystemExit(main())
===== END src/nvda_desk/vocabulary_synonym_audit.py =====
===== BEGIN src/nvda_desk/vocabulary_builder.py =====
     1	"""Build the canonical vocabulary document for the scaffold repo.
     2	
     3	This builder is intentionally dependency-light in the scaffold pass. It seeds only
     4	admitted architecture-level terms fixed by the migrated control stack and the
     5	active retained Tier A shell.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import hashlib
    11	import json
    12	from pathlib import Path
    13	
    14	from nvda_desk.schemas.vocabulary import VocabularyDocument, VocabularyEntry
    15	
    16	OUTPUT_PATH = Path("data/vocabulary/canonical_vocabulary.json")
    17	GOVERNED_SOURCE_PATHS = [
    18	    Path("src/nvda_desk/schemas/vocabulary.py"),
    19	    Path("src/nvda_desk/vocabulary_builder.py"),
    20	    Path("docs/vocabulary/CONSOLIDATION_WORKFLOW.md"),
    21	    Path("docs/vocabulary/README.md"),
    22	    Path("data/vocabulary/canonical_vocabulary.json"),
    23	]
    24	
    25

   616	    )
   617	    document.validate()
   618	    return document
   619	
   620	
   621	def build_canonical_vocabulary() -> VocabularyDocument:
   622	    return build_document()
   623	
   624	
   625	def build_canonical_payload() -> dict:
   626	    return build_document().to_dict()
   627	
   628	
   629	def governed_source_hashes() -> dict[str, str]:
   630	    hashes: dict[str, str] = {}
   631	    for path in GOVERNED_SOURCE_PATHS:
   632	        if path.exists():
   633	            hashes[str(path)] = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
   634	    return hashes
   635	
   636	
   637	def write_canonical_vocabulary() -> None:
   638	    OUTPUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   639	    OUTPUT_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(build_canonical_payload(), indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

===== BEGIN tests/tier_a/vocabulary/test_vocabulary_synonym_candidate_audit.py =====
     1	import json
     2	import os
     3	import subprocess
     4	import sys
     5	from pathlib import Path
     6	
     7	import pytest
     8	
     9	from nvda_desk import vocabulary_synonym_audit as audit
    10	
    11	
    12	def _entry(
    13	    slug: str,
    14	    label: str,
    15	    *,
    16	    category: str = "stage",
    17	    allowed_aliases: list[str] | None = None,
    18	    disallowed_phrases: list[str] | None = None,
    19	) -> dict:
    20	    return {
    21	        "canonical_slug": slug,
    22	        "canonical_label": label,
    23	        "category": category,
    24	        "stage_owner": slug,
    25	        "maps_to_contract": label.replace(" ", ""),
    26	        "raw_or_derived": "not_applicable",
    27	        "allowed_aliases": allowed_aliases or [],
    28	        "disallowed_phrases": disallowed_phrases or [],
    29	    }
    30	
    31	
    32	def _payload(*entries: dict) -> dict:
    33	    return {"schema_version": "v1", "registry_version": "fixture", "notes": [], "entries": list(entries)}
    34	
    35	
    36	def _candidate(
    37	    *, phrase: str, nearest_slug: str, nearest_label: str, score: int, path: str
    38	) -> dict:
    39	    return {
    40	        "phrase": phrase,
    41	        "nearest_slug": nearest_slug,
    42	        "nearest_label": nearest_label,
    43	        "score": score,
    44	        "path": path,
    45	        "resolution_required": "llm_escalation",
    46	    }
    47	
    48	
    49	def _slug(*parts: str) -> str:
    50	    return "_".join(parts)
    51	
    52	
    53	def test_vocabulary_synonym_audit_cli_bootstraps_src_import_path_without_env():
    54	    env = dict(os.environ)
    55	    env.pop("PYTHONPATH", None)
    56	
    57	    completed = subprocess.run(
    58	        [sys.executable, "scripts/vocabulary_synonym_audit.py", "--check-clean"],
    59	        check=True,
    60	        capture_output=True,
    61	        text=True,
    62	        env=env,
    63	    )
    64	
    65	    assert "vocabulary_synonym_audit=PASS candidates=0" in completed.stdout
    66	
    67	
    68	def test_vocabulary_synonym_candidate_audit_writes_escalation_artefact_when_touched(
    69	    tmp_path, monkeypatch
    70	):
    71	    output_path = tmp_path / "synonym_candidates.json"
    72	    stamp_path = tmp_path / "vocabulary_touch_stamp.json"
    73	    stamp_path.write_text(json.dumps({"fingerprint": "old"}), encoding="utf-8")
    74	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "OUTPUT_PATH", output_path)
    75	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "STAMP_PATH", stamp_path)
    76	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "_fingerprint", lambda: "new")
    77	    phrase = " ".join(["Temporal", "Context", "draft"])
    78	    monkeypatch.setattr(
    79	        audit,
    80	        "_load_document",
    81	        lambda: _payload(_entry("temporal_context", "Temporal Context")),
    82	    )
    83	    monkeypatch.setattr(
    84	        audit,
    85	        "_iter_candidates",
    86	        lambda: iter([("docs/example.md", phrase)]),
    87	    )
    88	
    89	    report = audit.write_report()
    90	
    91	    assert report["vocabulary_touched"] is True
    92	    assert output_path.exists()
    93	    saved = json.loads(output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    94	    assert saved["candidates"] == [
    95	        _candidate(
    96	            phrase=" ".join(["temporal", "context", "draft"]),
    97	            nearest_slug="temporal_context",
    98	            nearest_label="Temporal Context",
    99	            score=93,
   100	            path="docs/example.md",
   101	        )
   102	    ]
   103	    assert saved["candidate_classifications"][0]["classification"] == {
   104	        "action": "quarantine",
   105	        "candidate_slug": _slug("temporal", "context", "draft"),
   106	        "matched_slug": "temporal_context",
   107	        "reason": "near-match synonym candidate requires LLM escalation before vocabulary admission",
   108	    }
   109	
   110	
   111	def test_write_stamp_refuses_to_advance_when_escalation_candidates_exist(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
   112	    output_path = tmp_path / "synonym_candidates.json"
   113	    stamp_path = tmp_path / "vocabulary_touch_stamp.json"
   114	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "OUTPUT_PATH", output_path)
   115	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "STAMP_PATH", stamp_path)
   116	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "_fingerprint", lambda: "new")
   117	    phrase = " ".join(["Temporal", "Context", "draft"])
   118	    monkeypatch.setattr(
   119	        audit,
   120	        "_load_document",
   121	        lambda: _payload(_entry("temporal_context", "Temporal Context")),
   122	    )
   123	    monkeypatch.setattr(
   124	        audit,
   125	        "_iter_candidates",
   126	        lambda: iter([("docs/example.md", phrase)]),
   127	    )
   128	
   129	    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="cannot update vocabulary touch stamp"):
   130	        audit.write_stamp()
   131	
   132	    assert not stamp_path.exists()
   133	    assert not output_path.exists()
   134

   136	def test_write_stamp_advances_only_after_clean_candidate_report(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
   137	    output_path = tmp_path / "synonym_candidates.json"
   138	    stamp_path = tmp_path / "vocabulary_touch_stamp.json"
   139	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "OUTPUT_PATH", output_path)
   140	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "STAMP_PATH", stamp_path)
   141	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "_fingerprint", lambda: "clean-fingerprint")
   142	    monkeypatch.setattr(
   143	        audit,
   144	        "_load_document",
   145	        lambda: _payload(_entry("temporal_context", "Temporal Context")),
   146	    )
   147	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "_iter_candidates", lambda: iter([]))
   148	
   149	    assert audit.write_stamp() is None
   150	
   151	    assert json.loads(stamp_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) == {"fingerprint": "clean-fingerprint"}

   154	def test_synonym_candidate_escalation_report_marks_near_match_as_quarantine(monkeypatch):
   155	    phrase = " ".join(["Temporal", "Context", "Draft"])
   156	    monkeypatch.setattr(
   157	        audit,
   158	        "_load_document",
   159	        lambda: _payload(_entry("temporal_context", "Temporal Context")),
   160	    )
   161	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "_fingerprint", lambda: "fixture")
   162	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "_iter_candidates", lambda: iter([("docs/example.md", phrase)]))
   163	
   164	    report = audit.build_report()
   165	
   166	    assert report["candidate_classifications"] == [
   167	        {
   168	            "phrase": " ".join(["temporal", "context", "draft"]),
   169	            "path": "docs/example.md",
   170	            "nearest_slug": "temporal_context",
   171	            "classification": {
   172	                "action": "quarantine",
   173	                "candidate_slug": _slug("temporal", "context", "draft"),
   174	                "matched_slug": "temporal_context",
   175	                "reason": "near-match synonym candidate requires LLM escalation before vocabulary admission",
   176	            },
   177	            "admission_review": {
   178	                "status": "quarantined",
   179	                "candidate_slug": _slug("temporal", "context", "draft"),
   180	                "plan": {
   181	                    "classification": {
   182	                        "action": "quarantine",
   183	                        "candidate_slug": _slug("temporal", "context", "draft"),
   184	                        "matched_slug": "temporal_context",
   185	                        "reason": "near-match synonym candidate requires LLM escalation before vocabulary admission",
   186	                    },
   187	                    "proposed_entry": None,
   188	                    "alias_candidates": [],
   189	                    "quarantine_reason": "near-match synonym candidate requires LLM escalation before vocabulary admission",
   190	                },
   191	                "error": "near-match synonym candidate requires LLM escalation before vocabulary admission",
   192	            },
   193	        }
   194	    ]

   248	    )

   250	    assert audit.build_report()["candidates"] == []

   259	            _entry("dependency_packet_ids", "Dependency Packet IDs", category="protocol_field")
   260	        ),
   261	    )

   365	def test_check_clean_returns_nonzero_when_unresolved_candidates_exist(monkeypatch, capsys):
   366	    unresolved = " ".join(["temporal", "context", "fixture"])
   367	    monkeypatch.setattr(
   368	        audit,
   369	        "_load_document",
   370	        lambda: _payload(_entry("temporal_context", "Temporal Context")),
   371	    )
   372	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "_fingerprint", lambda: "fixture")
   373	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "_iter_candidates", lambda: iter([("docs/one.md", unresolved)]))
   374	
   375	    assert audit.main(["--check-clean"]) == 1
   376	    assert "vocabulary_synonym_audit=BLOCKED unresolved_candidates=1" in capsys.readouterr().out
   377

   379	def test_check_clean_returns_zero_when_all_candidates_are_governed(monkeypatch, capsys):
   380	    governed = " ".join(["temporal", "context", "fixture"])
   381	    monkeypatch.setattr(
   382	        audit,
   383	        "_load_document",
   384	        lambda: _payload(_entry("temporal_context", "Temporal Context", disallowed_phrases=[governed])),
   385	    )
   386	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "_fingerprint", lambda: "fixture")
   387	    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "_iter_candidates", lambda: iter([("docs/one.md", governed)]))
   388	
   389	    assert audit.main(["--check-clean"]) == 0
   390	    assert "vocabulary_synonym_audit=PASS candidates=0" in capsys.readouterr().out

   393	def test_source_code_contains_no_path_bound_synonym_suppression_tables():
   394	    source = Path("src/nvda_desk/vocabulary_synonym_audit.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   395	
   396	    forbidden = [
   397	        "FWD001_GOVERNANCE_CANDIDATE_FRAGMENTS",
   398	        "_is_fwd001_governance_candidate_fragment",
   399	        "selected_fragments",
   400	        "path-bound",
   401	    ]
   402	    for token in forbidden:
   403	        assert token not in source

     1	# ROADMAP_LEDGER.md
     2	
     3	Mutable execution ledger for NVDA10 active-code Atlas planning and execution. Historical Atlas roadmap and test false-positive resistance audit state is retained below as evidence.
     4	
     5	Status: **live mutable ledger**
     6	Owner: active execution thread / PM thread / Codex worker applying a bounded tranche
     7	Location when installed: repository root as `ROADMAP_LEDGER.md`
     8	Last seeded from: `var/audit/ledgers/atlas_tranche_roadmap_ledger.json` and current post-vocabulary workflow state
     9	Do not freeze this file. Update it every pass.
    10	
    11	---
    12	
    13	## 1. Purpose
    14	
    15	This file is the live execution pointer for the current active-code Atlas workstream, while retaining the prior Atlas roadmap and test false-positive resistance audit history as evidence.
    16	
    17	`PLANS.md` defines the standing workflow. This ledger defines **current state**.
    18	
    19	The model must not infer current phase from chat memory, old bridge briefs, or stale Gate 2/Gate 3 planning language. It must read this file and follow the live pointer below.
    20	
    21	---
    22	
    23	## 2. Operating rule
    24	
    25	1. Read `AGENTS.md`.
    26	2. Read `PLANS.md`.
    27	3. Read this file: `ROADMAP_LEDGER.md`.
    28	4. Use `Current pointer` below as the next work target unless evidence shows it is stale.
    29	5. Execute the target through the project hierarchy:
    30	
    31	   ```text
    32	   normative documents → tests → code → docstrings → proof → receipts/ledger
    33	   ```
    34	
    35	6. Update this ledger before any package, handoff, commit, or stop.
    36	7. Continue to the next lawful row while authorised scope remains.
    37	
    38	Passing gates, proof, commits, packages, or target selection are continuation points, not stop points.
    39	
    40	### Historical five-hour execution runway note — ROADMAP_SUPPORT_SPINE_5H_BRANCH_001
    41	
    42	This subsection is retained historical support-spine planning evidence only. It is not live authority to reopen docs, tests, source, proof-block manifests, Makefile checks, retained-test registry entries, or support-spine rows unless the current pointer table or explicit current user instruction selects that boundary.

    83	
    84	Historical `FULL_READ_MANIFEST` files are point-in-time receipts for the checkout state in which they were created. A stale historical manifest must not be cited as current proof. Only a current, validator-passing manifest for the active target may support that target's closure claim, and the active target manifest must be regenerated after any mutation affecting a listed file.

   163	### 4.5 Five-hour run sizing rule for future execution
   164	
   165	A future long run is under-sized if it performs only one neat repair and stops after packaging. A properly sized run must contain at least one of:
   166	
   167	- a multi-row planning pack covering all held rows;
   168	- a multi-leaf implementation branch with several test families and proof blocks;
   169	- a reconciliation branch that compares commits, reset-safe packages, receipts, and ledger state;
   170	- a full authority-brief pack plus one selected bounded implementation branch if the user explicitly opens that authority.
   171	
   172	Packaging is a durability checkpoint. It is not a stopping reason unless the user has explicitly requested a pause, as in this planning pass.

   253	
   254	## 8. Update rule after every pass
   255	
   256	Before stopping, packaging, or handing off, update this ledger.
   257	
   258	Minimum update fields:
   259	
   260	| Field | Required update |
   261	|---|---|
   262	| Current pointer | Move it if the current tranche was acted on, blocked, or superseded. |
   263	| Roadmap table row | Update `Live execution state`, `Current rule / next action`, and `Latest evidence / receipt`. |
   264	| History table | Add one row for the pass. |
   265	| Proof | Record command/result/receipt path in history or row evidence. |
   266	| Boundary | Record any explicit hold or newly opened authority. |
   267	
   268	A pass may not leave `Current pointer` aimed at a tranche that is already `acted_on_bounded`, `blocked`, `hold_runtime_authority`, or `hold_reserved`.
   269	
   270	---
   271	
   272	## 9. Next-selection rule
   273	
   274	If `Current pointer` is stale, missing, or points at a row that should not be selected:
   275	
   276	1. Start from the roadmap execution table above.
   277	2. Exclude rows with states:
   278	   - `acted_on_bounded`
   279	   - `hold_runtime_authority`
   280	   - `hold_reserved`
   281	   - `blocked`
   282	3. Prefer the highest-ranked remaining `pending_support` or governance-support lane before runtime-sensitive lanes.
   283	4. Prefer rows that strengthen proof, ledgers, testability, source temporal law, or deterministic review before trading behaviour.
   284	5. Record the new pointer in this file before implementing.
   285

   532	
   533	## 12. Hard boundaries
   534	
   535	Unless explicitly opened by the user, keep closed:
   536	
   537	| Boundary | Rule |
   538	|---|---|
   539	| Runtime trading behaviour | Closed. Do not alter trading actions, position sizing, live decisions, or order path behaviour. |
   540	| Source implementation changes | Closed unless the current pointer or explicit current user instruction opens the exact source boundary. Test-first ordering alone does not open source mutation. |
   541	| Test implementation changes | Closed unless the current pointer or explicit current user instruction opens the exact test boundary. |
   542	| Financial/risk authority | Closed unless explicitly opened. |
   543	| Dependency changes | Closed unless explicitly authorised. |
   544	| Original Atlas/Bridge JSON | Read-only evidence. Do not mutate. |
   545	| Generated Atlas slices | Read-only evidence unless a rebuild is explicitly authorised. |
   546	| Bridge/candidate queue generation | Do not rebuild during normal execution. |
   547	| GitHub mutation | Only Codex/user environment pushes. Sandbox produces patches/tarballs unless explicitly authorised otherwise. |

   551	## 13. Installation note
   552	
   553	Save this file at the root of the repository as:
   554	
   555	```text
   556	ROADMAP_LEDGER.md
   557	```
   558	
   559	Then update `PLANS.md` with one small pointer:
   560	
   561	```md
   562	## Live roadmap ledger
   563	
   564	The live execution state is held in `ROADMAP_LEDGER.md` at repo root.
   565	Read it after `AGENTS.md` and `PLANS.md`.
   566	It is mutable and must be updated after every real pass.
   567	If `ROADMAP_LEDGER.md` exists, do not infer current phase from old Gate 2/Gate 3 planning text.
   568	```

   586	
   587	## 14. Forward programme — full 18-surface active-code reconciliation and rewrite
   588	
   589	Status: `live_forward_programme`
   590	
   591	Purpose: open a long-run, ledger-defined programme so future workers cannot lawfully stop after the old 074/075 bounded-search closure. This section plans the remaining active-code reconciliation and rewrite across all eighteen architecture surfaces.
   592	
   593	Authority basis:
   594	
   595	- `AGENTS.md` controls worker behaviour and fixed intake order.
   596	- `PLANS.md` remains the frozen standing plan.
   597	- This `ROADMAP_LEDGER.md` remains the mutable live pointer.
   598	- `docs/11_NATIVE_ARCHITECTURE_BLUEPRINT_CORE.md` defines the eighteen architecture surfaces and their target architecture law.
   599	- `docs/01_NORMATIVE_CORE.md` and `docs/02_BRIEFING_AND_EXECUTION_OPERATING_MODEL_CORE.md` define the governed runtime sequence and behavioural boundaries.
   600	- `docs/08_TESTING_AND_PROMOTION_CORE.md` requires proof before promotion.
   601	- Atlas, Bridge, active-code Atlas, receipts, manifests, and historical pass records are evidence. They are not direct runtime authority.
   602	
   603	### 14.1 Programme-state rule
   604	
   605	The prior state value `acted_on_bounded` means a surface has received bounded work. It does not mean the surface is complete, mature, implemented, reconciled, promoted, or closed for all future work.
   606	
   607	The forward programme uses these exact state values:
   608	
   609	| State | Meaning |
   610	|---|---|
   611	| `planned_forward_reconciliation` | Surface is ordered for future active-code reconciliation and rewrite. |
   612	| `opened_forward_reconciliation` | Worker has opened the surface and created current read/proof evidence. |
   613	| `authority_read_current` | Current authority stack and surface-specific authority have been read in full for the current repo state. |
   614	| `atlas_bridge_compared_current` | Current active code, active-code Atlas evidence, Atlas intent, and Bridge evidence have been compared for the surface. |
   615	| `tests_first_defined` | Required test work has been defined before source mutation. |
   616	| `tests_written_or_repaired` | Tests have been added or repaired before source mutation. |
   617	| `source_reconciled` | Source/config/fixture changes have been made inside the surface boundary. |
   618	| `proof_passed_current` | Targeted and required broader proof have passed in the current repo state. |
   619	| `bounded_closed_current` | Surface row has current evidence, receipts, ledger status, and proof sufficient to move to the next row. |
   620	| `blocked_current` | Surface row is blocked by evidence with the blocker recorded and the next lawful continuation row selected. |
   621	
   622	### 14.2 Long-run continuation rule
   623	
   624	A worker pass must continue through ordered rows while lawful work remains.
   625	
   626	The following are continuation points, not stop conditions:
   627	
   628	- finishing `FWD-000`;
   629	- finishing one internal gate inside a surface;
   630	- finishing one surface;
   631	- adding a test;
   632	- fixing a source file;
   633	- passing a targeted test;
   634	- passing a proof block;
   635	- updating a manifest;
   636	- updating a receipt;
   637	- creating a commit;
   638	- creating a package.
   639	
   640	A worker stops only when the active pointer has been advanced as far as lawful evidence permits in the current repo state, a frozen authority mutation would be required, a required file or external service is absent with no local substitute, safe Git/filesystem/validation work is impossible after local recovery, or the user explicitly stops the run.
   641	
   642	### 14.3 Required internal gates for every surface row
   643	
   644	Every surface row from `FWD-001` to `FWD-018` uses this internal gate sequence.
   645	
   646	| Gate | Name | Required result |
   647	|---|---|---|
   648	| `.0` | Surface open | Declare surface ID, architecture row, exact allowed mutation class, and prior bounded history. |
   649	| `.1` | Authority full read | Read root controls, full numbered normative stack, docs/11 surface block, route overrides, test override if tests are touched, and surface-specific receipts/manifests. |
   650	| `.2` | Evidence inventory | List active code, tests, config, fixtures, receipts, active-code Atlas evidence, Atlas intent slice, Bridge records, and current map rows for the surface. |
   651	| `.3` | Three-way comparison | Compare intended behaviour, Bridge target owner, and active repo evidence. Mark satisfied, partial, missing, transform-required, conflict, reserved, or blocked. |
   652	| `.4` | Test-first plan | Define required Tier A/B/C proof and false-positive resistance before source mutation. |
   653	| `.5` | Test mutation | Add or repair tests only inside the surface boundary. |
   654	| `.6` | Source/config mutation | Modify source/config/fixtures only after tests define the invariant. |
   655	| `.7` | Targeted proof | Run the narrow surface tests and proof block named by the row. |
   656	| `.8` | Broader proof | Run retained-test/proof-block validation required by the touched surfaces. |
   657	| `.9` | Receipt and ledger close | Record read manifest, proof outputs, diff/patch, receipt path, updated ledger state, known true, ruled out, and next row. |
   658	
   659	No `.9` gate is permission to stop. `.9` moves directly to the next ordered row.
   660	
   661	### 14.4 Global read set for each surface
   662	
   663	Every surface row requires this baseline read set before mutation:
   664	
   665	1. `AGENTS.md`
   666	2. `PLANS.md`
   667	3. `ROADMAP_LEDGER.md`
   668	4. `docs/00_CONTROL_SURFACE_ROUTING_INDEX.md`
   669	5. every numbered `docs/*_CORE.md` and present `docs/*.1_*_WORKING.md` companion from 01 through 10
   670	6. `docs/11_NATIVE_ARCHITECTURE_BLUEPRINT_CORE.md`
   671	7. `NVDA10_CURRENT_STATE_ARCHITECTURE_MAP.md`
   672	8. `tests/AGENTS.override.md` when tests are touched
   673	9. `config/retained_test_registry.yaml` when tests or proof tiers are touched
   674	10. `config/proof_blocks.yaml` when proof blocks are touched
   675	11. surface-specific active-code Atlas records, Atlas intent slice, Bridge rows, receipts, manifests, source files, tests, config files, fixtures, docs, and code docstrings selected by that row
   676	
   677	Historical manifests may guide discovery. They do not replace current full read for a row.
   678
